    def gate(root, where: str, *, mailbox=None, now=None, defer_pending=False) -> Gate:
        """F0.6: the first step of every trial-bound command, before any callback, capture, provider request or pricing:
        the open trial (F6.3) and its ACK chain (F7.1) from the mailbox; the journal (chain, F0.5 step 1); the trial
        state (F1: INVALID refuses with no work, F0.6 - checked before the bundle so re-entry adds no repeat row); total
        loss (F6.6: INVALID (LOST)); the bundle against the AUDIT-ACK's (F6.4: refusal before any row, INVALID (bundle) +
        local abort after); the plan's trial_id (F6.2). The gate never creates or writes into a missing s1_out (review
        I-1). `now`: the command's clock callable (refusal records, the INVALID row's `at`). `defer_pending` (A3 Task 6,
        ruling N2, Erratum 1 E1.2): only the slot paths pass True - a stage whose lock is held by a live (possibly stalled)
        owner is recorded in gv.deferred instead of refusing "pending", so deadline arbitration is never blocked by a
        work lock."""
        root = Path(root)
        out = root / "s1_out"
        try:
            tr = open_trial(mailbox)                                        # mailbox only: closure is monotone (E3.4 r3)
            rows = j.read_trial(tr.trial_id, tr.genesis, mailbox)
        except (Refusal, j.JournalError) as e:
            if isinstance(e, (MailboxOutage, j.Outage)):
                _legacy_detectors(out, where, now, mailbox)                 # B3.4: an outage never downgrades a violation
            _refuse(out, where, str(e), now)
        probs = check_journal(tr, rows)
        if probs:
            _refuse(out, where, f"ACK journal binding {probs[:3]} (F7.1): escalate", now)
        st_ = state(tr, rows)
        if st_ == "INVALID":                                  # review M-3: before the bundle - no repeat INVALID rows
            bad = j.first(rows, "TRIAL-INVALID")
            if out.is_dir():                                  # (never recreates a lost s1_out)
                try:
                    c.write_abort(out, {"reason": f"journal INVALID ({bad.subject})", "first_invalid_line_sha256": bad.sha,
                                        "actual": None, "close": None, "scheduled": None, "projection_sha256": None,
                                        "receipt_sha256": None})
                except OSError:
                    pass
            raise Refusal(f"{where} refused: trial INVALID ({bad.subject}): no work (F0.6, F1)")
        if rows and not out.is_dir():                         # review I-1: total loss before the bundle (F6.6)
            invalidate(tr, "LOST", {"where": where, "missing": "s1_out"}, root=root, mailbox=mailbox, clock=now)
            raise Refusal(f"{where} refused: s1_out is gone under an open trial with journal rows: INVALID (LOST)")
        observed = c.bundle_sha256()
        if observed != tr.bundle:
            if not rows:
                _refuse(out, where, f"S1 bundle {observed[:12]} differs from the AUDIT-ACK's {tr.bundle[:12]} "
                                    f"(no journal row yet)", now)
            rec = {"where": where, "expected_bundle_sha256": tr.bundle, "observed_bundle_sha256": observed,
                   "problems": c.bundle_problems(c.bundle_manifest())}
            invalidate(tr, "bundle", rec, root=root, mailbox=mailbox, clock=now)
            c.write_abort(out, dict(rec, reason="bundle", actual=None, close=None, scheduled=None,   # (s1_out exists:
                                    projection_sha256=None, receipt_sha256=None))                     # loss checked)
>           raise Refusal(f"{where} refused: S1 bundle differs from the trial's AUDIT-ACK bundle after journal rows: "
                          f"INVALID (bundle)")

stage = 'audit'

    def run(gv, stage, *, inputs: dict, acks: dict, work, policy_fn=None, mailbox=None, clock=None,
            before_publish=None, extra_gate=None, stage_check=None) -> dict:
        """F4.1-F4.4 for one stage; re-entry resolves the existing rows per the stage table instead (never a second
        INTENT). A journal outage before the INTENT is a refusal with no request and no artifact (F4 failure 1).
        `extra_gate(rows) -> reason | None` is AND-ed with `permitted` at F0.5 step 5 (revision 2: introduced here,
        used by the seal stages and by Task 10); `stage_check(rows, subject, inputs) -> Decision | None` is evaluated
        at step 4a for the INTENT (E2.2 r3: a seal INTENT naming another QUALIFIED than the first one is INVALIDATE
        contradictory-stage). Both run inside the F0.1 transaction. A seal stage requires both (fail closed)."""
        clock = clock or c.now_utc
        stage_gate, check = _gates(gv, stage, extra_gate, stage_check)
        lk = _lock(gv, stage)
        try:
            rows = j.read_trial(gv.trial.trial_id, gv.trial.genesis, mailbox)
            m = stage_rows(rows, stage)
            if m["intent"] is not None:
                return _resolve(gv, stage, m, mailbox, clock)
            op, actor, held = j.new_nonce(), j.new_nonce(), {}
    
            def detail_fn(at):                                                    # OQ-7: the in-transaction clock
                held["policy"] = policy_fn(at) if policy_fn is not None else None
                held["envelope"] = envelope(gv.trial.trial_id, stage, inputs, acks, held["policy"])
                return j.detail_of(held["envelope"])
            try:
                intent = _need(_append(gv, j.Request("STAGE-INTENT", j.stage_subject(stage), op, actor,
                                                     detail_fn=detail_fn, inputs=dict(inputs)), mailbox, clock,
                                       stage_gate, check), stage, "STAGE-INTENT")
            except j.Outage as e:
                raise SystemExit(f"stage {stage}: journal outage before the INTENT ({e}): no request, no artifact") \
                    from None
            staging = _Staging(gv.root, gv.trial.trial_id, stage)
            c.publish_exclusive(staging.dir / "intent.json", c.canonical(held["envelope"]))       # OQ-35
            sw = StageWork(gv, stage, staging, intent, held["policy"], op, actor, mailbox, clock)
            try:
>               provenance = work(sw) or []

                    from None
            staging = _Staging(gv.root, gv.trial.trial_id, stage)

    def _get(client, auth, family, scope, url, params, headers, sw):
        """F4.6: one authorized request (refused BEFORE sending if outside the policy); its raw page is staged and
        recorded in provenance (credential-free: the headers are never recorded)."""
        n, rel = auth.authorize(family, scope, url, params)
        try:
            resp = client.get(url, params=params, headers=headers)
        except Exception as e:                                                # transport failure: no secrets in text
            auth.record(family, scope, n, url, params, c.now_utc().isoformat(), None, None, None)
            raise Structural(f"{rel}: transport error {type(e).__name__}") from None
        retrieved = c.now_utc()                                               # F4.5: the actual observation instant
        sw.put(rel, resp.content)

                    c.sha256_bytes(resp.content))

    def _validate_calendar(cal, plan, ack) -> list:
        """§4 + Task 12 (A6, A5.4): the provider listing passes s1_seal.calendar_problems (non-empty, ordered as listed,
        unique, open < close, covers the opening close through the deadline instant + the final lag); planned START ==
        the open instant of its date; >= 5 session opens strictly between the S1-AUDIT-ACK's recorded time (`ack`) and
        START. Returns [(date, open_utc)]."""

    def run(gv, stage, *, inputs: dict, acks: dict, work, policy_fn=None, mailbox=None, clock=None,

        """F4.1-F4.4 for one stage; re-entry resolves the existing rows per the stage table instead (never a second
        INTENT). A journal outage before the INTENT is a refusal with no request and no artifact (F4 failure 1).
        `extra_gate(rows) -> reason | None` is AND-ed with `permitted` at F0.5 step 5 (revision 2: introduced here,
        used by the seal stages and by Task 10); `stage_check(rows, subject, inputs) -> Decision | None` is evaluated
        at step 4a for the INTENT (E2.2 r3: a seal INTENT naming another QUALIFIED than the first one is INVALIDATE
        contradictory-stage). Both run inside the F0.1 transaction. A seal stage requires both (fail closed)."""
        clock = clock or c.now_utc
        stage_gate, check = _gates(gv, stage, extra_gate, stage_check)
        lk = _lock(gv, stage)
        try:
            rows = j.read_trial(gv.trial.trial_id, gv.trial.genesis, mailbox)
            m = stage_rows(rows, stage)
            if m["intent"] is not None:
                return _resolve(gv, stage, m, mailbox, clock)
            op, actor, held = j.new_nonce(), j.new_nonce(), {}
    
            def detail_fn(at):                                                    # OQ-7: the in-transaction clock
                held["policy"] = policy_fn(at) if policy_fn is not None else None
                held["envelope"] = envelope(gv.trial.trial_id, stage, inputs, acks, held["policy"])
                return j.detail_of(held["envelope"])
            try:
                intent = _need(_append(gv, j.Request("STAGE-INTENT", j.stage_subject(stage), op, actor,
                                                     detail_fn=detail_fn, inputs=dict(inputs)), mailbox, clock,
                                       stage_gate, check), stage, "STAGE-INTENT")
            except j.Outage as e:
                raise SystemExit(f"stage {stage}: journal outage before the INTENT ({e}): no request, no artifact") \
                    from None
            staging = _Staging(gv.root, gv.trial.trial_id, stage)
            c.publish_exclusive(staging.dir / "intent.json", c.canonical(held["envelope"]))       # OQ-35
            sw = StageWork(gv, stage, staging, intent, held["policy"], op, actor, mailbox, clock)

>               provenance = work(sw) or []

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("day", ["2026-10-23", "2026-11-27", "2027-05-14"])
    def test_m1_a_session_closing_before_the_earliest_early_close_refuses_the_audit(tmp_path, day):
        """Re-review 8 m-1: the clock anchor assumes no session closes before EARLIEST_CLOSE_NY (13:00 America/New_York).
        A fetched provider listing with any session in the plan's calendar_range closing earlier (here 12:59, on the
        opening close, a mid-trial day and the last week of calendar_range) makes the audit fail closed: the attempt is preserved as
        ABORTED (Structural, the reason recorded), no COMPLETE manifest exists, so nothing ACK-dependent can follow."""
        cl = audit_with_calendar(tmp_path, _with_close(day, "12:59"))
        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):

_ test_h3_probe_a_session_inserted_into_the_calendar_after_the_opening_seal_aborts[2026-10-24] _

tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/gl450/AppData/Local/Temp/pytest-of-gl450/pytest-21157/test_h3_probe_a_session_insert0')
fake = '2026-10-24'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("fake", ["2026-10-24", "2026-10-25"])
    def test_h3_probe_a_session_inserted_into_the_calendar_after_the_opening_seal_aborts(tmp_path, fake):
        """H-3 reviewer probe test_calendar_insert_after_opening (B3.3): after the opening seal (before any RUN-ACK) a
        fake session is inserted into audit/calendar.json between the real opening close and START. The opening close
        comes from plan.json verified against the AUDIT-ACK's plan_sha256 and the anchor never reads calendar.json, so the
        next opening slot records the persistent "registration artifact" abort (the calendar is bound by the audit
        manifest); restoring calendar.json does not resume: the START attestation is refused and the abort stays."""
        start, cal, oc, _ = s1_plan_audit(tmp_path, START)
        lg = Ledger(tmp_path / "l.sqlite3")
        assert s1_opening_checkpoint(tmp_path, lg, oc, cal, S1_RECEIPT)["seal"]["written"]
        out = tmp_path / "s1_out"
        cp = out / "audit" / "calendar.json"
        good = cp.read_bytes()
        rows = json.loads(good)

    def test_h3_probe_a_session_inserted_into_the_calendar_after_the_opening_seal_aborts(tmp_path, fake):
        """H-3 reviewer probe test_calendar_insert_after_opening (B3.3): after the opening seal (before any RUN-ACK) a
        fake session is inserted into audit/calendar.json between the real opening close and START. The opening close
        comes from plan.json verified against the AUDIT-ACK's plan_sha256 and the anchor never reads calendar.json, so the
        next opening slot records the persistent "registration artifact" abort (the calendar is bound by the audit
        manifest); restoring calendar.json does not resume: the START attestation is refused and the abort stays."""

        lg = Ledger(tmp_path / "l.sqlite3")

        good = cp.read_bytes()
        rows = json.loads(good)
        rows.append({"date": fake, "open": "09:30", "close": "16:00"})
        rows.sort(key=lambda x: x["date"])
        cp.write_text(json.dumps(rows), encoding="utf-8")

        with _Clock(act), pytest.raises(SystemExit):
            ck.run_opening_checkpoint(tmp_path, lg.path, sch, lambda: S1_RECEIPT, lambda: dict(S1_PROCESS),
                                      tmp_path / "crash.log", now=lambda: act)

    def _decide_locked(gv, stage, m) -> None:
        """The stage-table decision on rows re-read under the stage lock (F5.1)."""
        import s1_trial
        if m["created"] is not None:
            _check_created(gv, stage, m)
            return
        if _is_seal(stage) and m["intent"] is not None and m["prepared"] is None \
                and (gv.out / single_member(gv.out, stage)).exists():
            s1_trial.invalidate(gv.trial, "registration-artifact", {"stage": stage, "problem": "seal file with no "
                                "PREPARED"}, root=gv.root, mailbox=gv.mailbox)
            raise SystemExit(f"stage {stage}: seal file with no PREPARED: INVALID (registration-artifact)")
        if m["intent"] is None:
            present = _artifacts_present(gv.out, stage)
            if present:
                s1_trial.invalidate(gv.trial, "registration-artifact", {"stage": stage, "present": present},
